# AppContinuation ohosTest 用例说明

## 状态与边界

**全部待运行。** 这批修改未编译、未构建、未做类型检查或transpile、未运行应用测试或真机验证；不能把源码中断言当成已通过的结果。下表包含源码中的437个不同 `@tc.number` 定义（26个套件文件），不是执行次数或覆盖率。

## 执行前提与方式（供后续获授权时使用，本次未执行）

1. 准备匹配SDK26.0.0的DevEco Studio/SDK和已授权设备，配置使用者自己的调试签名。
2. 通过IDE的ohosTest入口加载 [List.test.ets](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/List.test.ets)，可用表中实际用例名过滤单例。
3. 纯模型用例使用注入时钟/存储/原生接口；这不能证明真实跨设备系统行为。NativeQa用例需要真实专用Preferences适配器；UiQa用例需要UiTest Driver和FeatureQaAbility，均使用隔离命名空间/夹具。
4. 保留 [BoundedCase.ets](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/support/BoundedCase.ets) 的scenario/watchdog：场景10秒看门狗，runner配置12秒。begin/failure/end只在公共入口记录，不对每条断言重复打印，失败必须原样抛给Hypium。
5. 后续实际运行时分别记录设备/系统、语言、SDK/工具链、用例编号、真实结果和日志路径；未运行不得填写PASS。双设备系统入口另行人工验证，不与受控用例混淆。

## 人工检查（无伪造自动编号）

- 英文与zh_CN分别重启Ability，检查首页、三业务页面、对话框、备份预览、模型摘要及失败提示，特别是动态占位符。
- 双设备正常接续和快速拉起分别检查：系统结果/数据/窗口/实际定位全部到齐前不提交；冲突接受/拒绝不得静默覆盖本机。
- 本地备份勾选范围、取消、失败补偿和冻结后的救援导出，检查未选场景和未保存草稿。

## 逐例编号、步骤与预期

“准备/操作”与“断言摘录”来自对应源码，长用例以源链接中的完整过程及全部断言为准；未抽取到独立动作的纯函数用例按源链接构造输入并调用被测函数。列出的断言仅为预期，不表示执行成功。

| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_0600](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L153)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0006` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('empty');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPreview');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupApply');` | Confirmed restoration commits the production root and updates note plus reading state instead of fabricating a UI success.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('本地业务工作区已恢复。');`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('note').noteBody).assertEqual('qa-private-backup-body');`<br>`expect(reading.state.reading.chapter).assertEqual(1);`<br>`expect(fixture.store.flushCalls).assertEqual(fixture.baselineFlushes + 1);` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_0700](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L186)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0007` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('empty');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPreview');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupApply');` | A scene edit while the confirmation is open invalidates its captured token and preserves the newer local draft.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('恢复预览已过期，请重新校验并预览。');`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('note').noteBody).assertEqual('确认期间的新正文');`<br>`expect(fixture.store.stored()).assertEqual(fixture.baseline);`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupApply')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_0800](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L210)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0008` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('writeFailure');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPreview');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupApply');` | A failed flush is compensated by the real transaction and the panel consumes the failed preview instead of claiming recovery.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('场景保存失败，已补偿回原存档；编辑、修订与历史保持原样');`<br>`expect(fixture.store.stored()).assertEqual(fixture.baseline);`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('note').noteBody).assertEqual('本机原正文');`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneWorkbench.snapshot().storageFrozen).assertFalse();` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_0900](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L234)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0009` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('frozen');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate');` | Frozen storage permits a memory rescue export but gates preview and restore without overwriting corrupt bytes.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate')).isEnabled()).assertTrue();`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupPreview')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('已生成当前内存救援副本；原存档仍冻结，未修复或覆盖磁盘。');`<br>`expect(currentWorkspaceBackupUiQa().store.stored()).assertEqual('{');` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1000](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L254)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0010` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('empty');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPreview');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, '{}');` | Editing input after a valid preview revokes authorization before the user attempts a restore.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupApply')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupSummary')).assertEqual('尚未预览；校验和取消均不会写入业务存储。');`<br>`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('导入文本已变更，请校验并预览后再恢复。');`<br>`expect(fixture.store.flushCalls).assertEqual(fixture.baselineFlushes);` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1100](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L275)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0011` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('empty');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPreview');` | Collapsing and reopening clears sensitive export and import buffers without clearing the real workspace.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupImportText')).assertEqual('');`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupCopy')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupApply')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('note').noteBody).assertEqual('本机原正文');` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1200](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L299)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0012` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('empty');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupCopy');` | Cancelling the production privacy dialog never invokes even the isolated clipboard and preserves the generated export.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('已取消。');`<br>`expect(fixture.clipboard.texts.length).assertEqual(0);`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupCopy')).isEnabled()).assertTrue();`<br>`expect(fixture.store.stored()).assertEqual(fixture.baseline);` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1300](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L321)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0013` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('copyPending');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupCopy');`<br>`throw new Error('Backup clipboard acknowledgment not rendered');` | Confirmed copy awaits the actual model clipboard promise, gates input and sends the complete export rather than its short preview.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('正在等待本机剪贴板复制结果…');`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupImportText')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(fixture.clipboard.texts[0]).assertEqual(exported);` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1400](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L351)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0014` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('copyFailure');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupCopy');` | A clipboard rejection is redacted by the production model, leaves input intact and re-enables retry without a scene write.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('备份复制失败；业务数据和输入保持不变。');`<br>`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupImportText')).assertEqual(fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupCopy')).isEnabled()).assertTrue();`<br>`expect(fixture.store.stored()).assertEqual(fixture.baseline);` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1500](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L378)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0015` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('empty');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeNote');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeReading');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeForm');` | Deselecting every real scope checkbox gates only import operations and never disables a safe full export.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupPreview')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupApply')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupGenerate')).isEnabled()).assertTrue();`<br>`expect(fixture.store.stored()).assertEqual(fixture.baseline);` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1600](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L403)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0016` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('scopePreserve');`<br>`throw new Error('Local saved baselines were invalid');`<br>`throw new Error('Saved unselected scenes missing');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeReading');` | Note-only restoration preserves unselected reading/form edits in memory without silently persisting those dirty values.<br>断言摘录：`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('note').noteBody).assertEqual('qa-private-backup-body');`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('reading').readingChapter).assertEqual(2);`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('form').formName).assertEqual('本机未保存姓名');`<br>`expect(JSON.stringify(reading)).assertEqual(JSON.stringify(beforeReading));` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1700](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L450)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0017` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('empty');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPreview');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeForm');` | Changing scope revokes an already valid preview while retaining source input for an explicit new review.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('恢复范围已变更，请重新校验并预览。');`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupApply')).isEnabled()).assertFalse();`<br>`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupImportText')).assertEqual(fixture.incomingRaw);`<br>`expect(await (await backupQaNode(driver, 'ACBackupScopeForm')).isChecked()).assertFalse();` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1800](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L472)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0018` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('scopePreserve');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeReading');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeForm');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, encodeWorkspace(emptyWorkspace()));` | A selected scene absent from a valid backup is explicitly cleared, while unselected dirty scenes and their saved roots survive.<br>断言摘录：`expect((await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupSummary')).indexOf('清空') &gt;= 0).assertTrue();`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('note').noteBody).assertEqual('');`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('form').formName).assertEqual('本机未保存姓名');`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('reading').readingDirty).assertTrue();` | 待运行 |
| [SUB_CONTINUATION_WORKSPACEBACKUPUIQA_1900](entry/src/ohosTest/ets/test/WorkspaceBackupUiQa.test.ets#L507)<br>`testWorkspaceBackupUiQa0019` | `const driver = await launchWorkspaceBackupUiQa('scopePreserve');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupPanelToggle');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeReading');`<br>`await backupQaClick(driver, 'ACBackupScopeForm');`<br>`await backupQaInput(driver, fixture.incomingRaw);` | A selective transaction failure compensates the saved root and preserves both selected original data and unselected volatile edits.<br>断言摘录：`expect(await backupQaText(driver, 'ACBackupStatus')).assertEqual('场景保存失败，已补偿回原存档；编辑、修订与历史保持原样');`<br>`expect(fixture.store.stored()).assertEqual(fixture.baseline);`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('note').noteBody).assertEqual('本机原正文');`<br>`expect(fixture.controller.sceneSnapshot('reading').readingChapter).assertEqual(2);` | 待运行 |
